# RH solution-object ledger (universal-law field)

What any proof of RH, and the missing object it needs, MUST be/have/do and CANNOT be/have/do, distilled from audited theorems, no-gos and RH-equivalent receivers. Facts only: no history, work log, route status or task list.

Conventions: each bullet is one constraint (MUST/CANNOT) or one proved fact (F…) with its consequence; E = evidence. Tags: [L] Lean-certified, [P] paper proof only, [Ls] Lean scratch only, [N] numerical only. ¬ZL = gives no zero-location/RH conclusion. Receiver = proved implication/equivalence ending in RH whose premise is unproved; producer = a proof of such a premise. Original IDs are kept for lookup; `r` marks a later entry that reused an earlier number. Paths: R:X = formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/X.lean; H:X = formal/GaloisForLFunctions/HTGeometry/X.lean; ML:X = formal/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/X.lean; Pn:x = docs/drafts/pipeline/{0-backlog,1-in-progress,2-fully-proven,3-formalized,4-publication-ready,5-failed}[n]/x.md; ULF:x = P1:universal-law-field/x.

## RH: route-independent research constraints

- CANNOT read high-frequency Xi zero location from any finite ordinary inverse-power expansion in the critical strip. Literal theta source has a positive renewal factorization whose two-ended phase = expected sine integral of a source-defined probability law; its survival function extends evenly through the seam ⇒ every algebraic endpoint coefficient of the positive-frequency phase gap vanishes, while positive real-frequency Xi zeros = exactly zeros of that gap. On every closed inner strip `|Im z|≤b<1/4`, `Xi(2z)` = `O(e^(−κ|Re z|))` ∀ `0<κ<π/2` ([L] uniform bound: R:XiHorizontalStripDecay). MUST control the global source-dependent remainder, not infer zero location from a finite high-frequency jet. [P] Exact phase representation: P2:theta/theta-renewal-phase-cosh-factorization; exponentially flat loop + resonance-phase reading: P2:theta/theta-renewal-complex-resonance-stieltjes-obstruction; neither proves RH.
- CANNOT encode the full Xi critical divisor by a nonzero fixed strip-holomorphic almost-periodic scalar observation, or a finite determinant of such. Absolutely convergent Dirichlet Fourier series with positive exponential-frequency moment has uniform finite real-zero count per unit interval ⇒ `O(T)` distinct real zeros to height `T`; unconditional positive proportion of simple critical Xi zeros gives `≫ T log T` distinct critical ordinates. Same for determinants convergent in one fixed positive weighted-Wiener algebra. Divisor construction MUST lose that fixed aperture via a singular limit or use a different source class; bounded seam transport alone gives no divisor. [P] route instance: P2:stc-physical-ordinate-transport-singularity#zero-density-rigidity-of-every-fixed-bounded-rigged-reading. Necessity statement, not RH.
- MUST retain the critical cusp boundary charge when completing literal theta Euler sources. Every individual Gaussian source atom and every fixed finite-prime smooth-number source has zero integral against `exp(x/2)`; completed theta source has integral `1/2` ⇒ finite-prime sources cannot converge in that weighted `L¹` topology. Against any bounded `C¹` observation `ψ` with integrable derivative their limit = completed bulk observation − `(1/2)ψ(−∞)`; same for every cofinal exhaustion by atom sets with finite reciprocal mass per stage, any ordering. [P] P2:theta-euler-critical-boundary-mass-escape (no RH assumed). Xi spectral exponentials unbounded at that cusp ⇒ full complex boundary response still needs an arithmetic orientation law; scalar half-charge cannot locate zeros. Source completion MUST carry that singular boundary observation, not exchange Euler limit with critical Mellin integral.
- MUST treat both reflected critical ports as a singular coupled completion. Every finite-prime theta Euler stage: `+1/2` Mellin value = 0, first derivative = `1/2` after multiplication by finite Euler factor at `1`; at `−1/2` meromorphic pole of order = number of admitted primes; completed source has finite charge `1/2` at both ports. Any scalar meromorphic repair making both finite-stage ports nonzero introduces a simple pole at `+1/2` and a zero of that growing order at `−1/2`. [P] P2:theta-euler-two-port-jet-pole-renormalization. Regular scalar limit or one endpoint jet cannot supply full complex-phase orientation.
- CANNOT infer Xi-divisor continuity from local convergence of literal theta sources, even with one critical boundary charge converging. Deleting any finite prime set preserves every interior Xi zero with multiplicity (Mellin transform = Xi × zero-free finite Euler factors). Deleting all primes leaves the Gaussian seed compact-locally; seed has no interior Xi zero. Along this path half-density charge → seed's zero charge, but every real interior Mellin observation of deleted sources → 0 while seed's is strictly negative. [P] P2:theta-prime-deletion-spectral-escape. MUST control full complex boundary family + actual positivity/modular source, not just local convergence, reflection, or one scalar endpoint charge.
- CANNOT exclude off-line zeros in the natural Xi strip from positivity, evenness, strong log-concavity, any finite moment list, exact critical half-density charge, and all finite prime-deletion right-ray positivity inequalities. At every high horizontal frequency and fixed strip height, a two-packet perturbation forces a zero preserving shape properties, ordinary mass, both reflected charges, converging to literal theta source in Schwartz topology. Version 1: = theta outside a fixed compact interval, positive after every finite literal prime deletion on positive ray, uniformly over prime sets. Version 2: real-analytic, converges holomorphically on compacts of theta source strip, relative tail error faster than every ordinary exponential in log coordinate. [P] P2:theta-compact-critical-strip-zero-engineering. Neither preserves exact Euler coefficients or entire real-zero divisor; does not identify which infinite source constraint can orient Xi.
- CANNOT infer critical-line zeros, a zero-free strip, or a local zero-count bound from global strict reflected Riccati/correlation-angle curvature, even with positive even strongly log-concave source and order-one entire Fourier growth. Symmetric three-Gaussian mixture: uniform positive two-variable Riccati margin, arbitrarily many off-real zeros in any prescribed real interval and any strip `0<Im z<η`; hyperbolic-cosine damping keeps selected zeros, enforces order one ([P] P2:theta-global-riccati-order-one-offline-countermodel). CANNOT repair by also fixing ordinary mass and both reflected critical half-density charges: charge-fitted variant matches those three scalar budgets of literal completed theta exactly, keeps strict global Riccati law + order-one growth, has off-line zero arbitrarily close to critical line ([P] P2:theta-riccati-three-charge-offline-countermodel). Neither preserves exact theta Euler coefficients; zero exclusion MUST control a nonlocal arithmetic phase beyond scalar/differential laws.
- CANNOT create a new Xi zero by symmetric integer-scale deformation preserving theta source's ordinary mass, critical half-density charge and complete monotonicity of its normalized flux — even for signed infinite mixtures of all integer translations. Exact Gaussian-rate arithmetic turns flux positivity into nonnegative weights at every reduced rational denominator; strictly positive divisor-Möbius identity forces deformation = identity. [P] P2:theta-divisor-bernstein-gauge-rigidity. Scope: that deformation class; literal one-sided Euler completion still has boundary-mass defect needing its own complex orientation law.
- CANNOT orient Xi zeros from: all finite-prime-deleted fluxes completely monotone + global source positivity + ordinary mass + one exact half-density charge without the reflected charge. Positive two-shift theta mixture has all these and an exact off-line Mellin zero at real strip coordinate `1/4`. Cofinal prime deletion isolates Gaussian seed; all-sieve flux monotonicity ⇔ positivity of an arbitrary exponentially integrable translation measure; with ordinary mass and both reflected half-charges fixed, class rigidifies to theta. Symmetric positive mixtures with charge defect `O(T^(−2))` can add off-line zeros at height `T` when shift gap shrinks. [P] P2:theta-all-sieve-seed-projector-translation-cone (classification + sharp tests). Completion proof MUST keep the second singular boundary observation and control moving spectral height, not only finite-sieve positivity + approximate charge convergence.
- MUST verify both sides of the moment cone when deforming a nonnegative finite spectral carrier. Fixing one atom `x` in an `N`-atom head of mass `E`, quadratic energy `F`: remaining atoms need `(E-x)²/(N-1) ≤ F-x² ≤ (E-x)²`; Cauchy lower bound alone does not certify nonnegative atoms. [L] R:FiniteOneHeadResidualWindow (positive interval only after separate upper-bound check). ¬ZL.
- MUST match complete padded carrier vectors at support-count seams before an intermediate-value argument; equal moments on neighbouring branches do not give a continuous atom choice. At integer effective rank `k`, reverse branch with `k` equal atoms `A/k` agrees coordinatewise after padding with neighbouring `(k+1)` branch (extra atom 0). [L] R:FiniteReverseResidualSelector (continuity from seam identities where residual mass and energy positive); [L] R:FiniteReverseResidualBranchMoments (nonnegative atoms, exact first two moments when selected rank within finite slot count).
- MUST supply an all-order source representation or independent divisor identity beyond any finite-jet carrier. [L] Nonnegative finite head matches completed source's first three relevant power sums exactly, unconditionally (R:XiPrimeMarkedFiniteSixthExactCarrier). [L] Every fixed finite Hankel block has infinitely many unrestricted moment-sequence extensions (H:FiniteHankelBoundaryExtensionClass) ⇒ finite data do not fix later moments. [L] Representing every canonical source moment by one positive measure ⇒ RH (R:PrimeArchMomentMeasureRH); the finite match gives neither that representation nor Xi's divisor.
- CANNOT derive the first open source Hankel minor from local zero geometry, RVM counting asymptotics, and positivity of every individual moment at every order. Abstract nodes `1+iε`, `1-iε`, `2` (unit weight, `0<ε<1`): `M_n=∑λ^(n+1)` has `M₀=4`, `M₀M₂-M₁²=4(1-ε⁴)>0`, size-three determinant `-8ε²(1+ε²)³<0`; `|1+iε|<√2` ⇒ real node `2` dominates conjugate pair at every power `n+1≥2` ⇒ every `M_n>0`; satisfies the source's three known strict cold orientations `M₂<M₀³/4`, `M₃<M₀⁴/8`, `M₄<M₀⁵/16`. Rescaling by `0<c<1/300`, choosing `0<ε<√c`, doubling weights preserves these signs, puts reciprocal nodes inside proved `1/150` radius and inverse-square-root spectral coordinates inside Xi strip with FE–Schwarz pairing. Appending a late positive real reciprocal-RVM tail preserves strict signs and gives smooth RVM count up to bounded error. Actual Xi needs a global arithmetic relation among nodes; model says nothing about Xi's determinant. E: P1:k-rvm-finite-sixth-moment-bracket (signed triple expansion, tail completion).
- CANNOT settle the first open source Hankel minor from decreasing source density + low-order radius-moment signs. Positive radius-mixing Gram inequality gives exact fifth-moment floor, but a shape-normalized Gaussian perturbation has strictly negative four-moment determinant gate while first moment, effective-rank gap, ordinary and radius shifted minors, adjacent-ratio separation stay positive ([L] algebraically: R:PrimeArchRadiusGaussianGateBoundary; realization by smooth decreasing density = paper reconstruction). At Gaussian boundary floor loses `840/11` of fifth moment while prime–archimedean determinant vanishes. MUST control actual theta fifth moment jointly with lower-moment deviations.
- CANNOT get a positive critical-zero head from the current initial off-line-exclusion band: exclusion says zeros of squared ordinate below `2365/16` would be critical; separate source radius puts every Xi zero above `599/4=2396/16` ⇒ band contains no zero. Finite-critical-head argument MUST locate critical zeros beyond it and control remaining divisor tail; tail margin explicit in P1:k-rvm-finite-sixth-moment-bracket.
- CANNOT infer the first open reciprocal-moment Hankel determinant from global source log-concavity + order-one entire Fourier transform, or from theta-cusp tail + strict raw-moment log-convexity. Symmetric probability source `(1-A)δ₀+(A/2)(δ₋₁+δ₁)` at `A=49/100`: all first five cumulant-derived moments positive, `M₀M₂-M₁²>0`, leading size-three determinant `A³(945A²+630A-551)/4572288000<0`. Gaussian convolution with variance > 1 makes it globally strictly log-concave and lowers the determinant further; multiplying density by small `exp(-ε cosh(2x))` keeps signs, gives order-one entire transform. Smooth compact convolution + small admixture of normalized actual theta-cusp source gives smooth strictly positive full-support source = positive multiple of actual source outside a compact set, same determinant sign. Neither = actual modular theta-cusp source. MUST prove source-specific quantitative cross-moment law. E: P1:k-rvm-finite-sixth-moment-bracket.
- CANNOT infer the first open determinant from superexponential likelihood separation among source atoms. Any smooth positive full-support even density `f`, all moments finite, negative first open determinant (as above): on positive half-line `f₁=f`, `f_n(x)=δ 2^(-n) f(x) exp(-π(n²-1)exp(2x))`, `n≥2`. ∀ `0<m≤n`, `0<x≤y`: `f_n/f_m` satisfies equality in theta Morse-gap contraction `r(y)=exp(-π(n²-m²)(exp(2y)-exp(2x)))r(x)`; later atoms sum ≤ `δ f` ⇒ first six half-line even moments → those of `f` as `δ→0`; normalized cumulant determinant stays negative for small `δ>0`. Exact pairwise contraction does not control dominant atom shape. MUST also get a quantitative inequality for actual complete source or dominant atom + tail interaction; model does not satisfy actual theta atom formulas.
- CANNOT infer the first open determinant from an all-order linear upper moment recurrence + strict shifted Hankel positivity. Symmetric three-point negative-determinant law (mass `A=49/100` at `±1`) rescaled to support `[-r,r]` (every prime–archimedean moment homogeneous in even power of `r` ⇒ negative size-three determinant keeps sign); add small symmetric smooth bump, positive on an interval, support `[-R,R]`, `r<R<√(17/303)`: determinant stays negative for small bump mass; every finite shifted raw-moment Hankel matrix strictly positive; compact support ⇒ `t_(j+1)≤R²t_j` ⇒ `303t_(j+1)≤(32j+17)t_j` ∀ `j≥0`. - MUST retain harmonic powers and time moments of the matched source observable. Finite sum of exponential modes cannot reproduce exponential × nonconstant affine time factor (free or repeated frequencies); such a factor can arise from differentiating the source pairing without a multiple arithmetic zero or defective physical mode. Replacement MUST keep that source information; fixed-amplitude fitting outside these obstructions.
- CANNOT identify zeta with a carrier having a fixed nonzero translation period in the unchanged complex coordinate. `zeta(x+a)=zeta(x)` for all large real `x` forces `a=0`; primitive `F` with `F'=zeta` on a right half-plane has `F(z+a)=F(z)+b` there only if `a=b=0`. MUST distinguish period of the function from periodic pattern among zeros (not excluded). ¬ZL.
- CANNOT recover continued Dirichlet values from a single complex algebra evaluation defined on every coefficient family with some finite absolute-convergence abscissa: every such evaluation = `f -> f(1)`, no continuity/shift-invariance assumption; algebra contains canonical formal inverse of every family with `f(1)!=0`, forcing scalar classification. Does not assert inverse keeps original abscissa; does not exclude evaluation on smaller fixed domains.
- CANNOT recover Dirichlet-series zero information from a scalar linear readout invariant under a fixed shift of every finite-abscissa family: complex shift `a`, `Re(a)>0`, readout with `tau(n^(-a)f(n))=tau(f)` ⇒ `tau(f)=f(1)*tau(1)`; exact inverse `f(n)/(n^(-a)-1)` preserves every point of absolute convergence, keeps coefficient-one remainder. - CANNOT infer a zeta zero from disagreement between fixed arithmetic orderings of its alternating series. Rounds of `a` odd then `b` even terms, fixed positive integers: complete-round sums converge on `0<Re(s)<1` ⇔ `a=b`, else magnitudes → ∞; retained endpoint `2^(-s)(a^(1-s)-b^(1-s))*N^(1-s)/(1-s)`; subtracting it recovers `(1-2^(1-s))*zeta(s)` for every quota. [L] R:EtaArithmeticRoundConvergence. Ordering-dependent divergence occurs at nonzeros too; variable quotas and other summation procedures need own analysis.
- CANNOT create new multiplicative relations inside the critical strip via nonzero unconditional products of integer powers of `p^(-s)`: for `Re(s)!=0`, product over finite prime subsets has nonzero complex limit ⇔ only finitely many nonzero exponents; limit = 1 only for empty word. Euler factors, grouped/renormalized limits, additive Dirichlet sums not classified; default-value convention ≠ convergence or relation.

## Hard facts

- F01 Receiver abundance: many exact RH equivalences in corpus (positivity, flatness, finite action, recurrence descent, formation properness, source-dagger descent, boundary absence) = readouts, not independent evidence or producers.
- HARD LOCK P: positivity alone does not supply the missing arithmetic inequality. All-test Weil positivity RH⇔; generic finite Gram/CP/KMS constructions nonselective; stated positive Green–Stokes split exceeds its Euler budget (no-gos keep exact premises). Independently proved quantitative bound for the literal arithmetic source remains a possible producer; being positive, quadratic or contractive is not a disqualification. Gamma–Hankel PSD gives a lower covariance floor, not the missing upper ceiling (`MobiusLaplaceGammaHankelWall`: `finiteMobiusGammaHankelOffDiagonalCovariance_lower_floor`).
- F02 Finite local exactness nonselective: arbitrary finite packets, jets, multiplicities, indices, Grams, KMS systems, homology, determinants, recoveries coexist with off-line charge.
- CANNOT infer real zeros for the Fourier transform of a positive, even, double-exponentially decaying source `(H''-H/4)/2` from positive definiteness of mass log curvature `(log H)''`; these coexist with off-real zeros; sign transfer needs more structure. P5:clg-ttf-theta-power-curvature-transfer ([P] counterexample; [L] positive family).
- F03 MUST distinguish identities between functions from relations at one point: differential independence excludes nonzero polynomial identities in functions + derivatives over the coefficient field, not a common zero; finite family analytic at `a`, differentially independent over a field ⊇ `C(s)`: multiplying every function by `s-a` preserves independence, makes every value at `a` zero ⇒ independence cannot retire a relation forced only at a zeta zero. [L] `HTGeometry.differentialIndependent_mulBase_iff`; zeta-dilation application `AdjacentDilation.adjacentDilationGauge_differentialIndependent_over_zeta`. Relation at a zero needs own arithmetic proof.
- F04 Source/receiver firewall: a construction can land exactly on Xi data while its final continuity, sign, descent, properness or bound is still literally RH.
- CANNOT count a positivity identity with explicit `|Xi|²` factor as a source proof of strict positivity: centered coordinate `E(w)=(1/2+w)X(w)`: `E+E#=X`, `|E(w)|²-|E#(w)|²=2 Re(w)|X(w)|²`; defect vanishes exactly at the off-line zeros to be excluded ⇒ strictness MUST come from source-derived nonvanishing independent of this factor. P2:theta-half-line-port-alternating-orientation#additive-ports-need-a-nondegeneracy-gate.
- F05 Large fused subcorpus has one signed current `a(s) = 2 Re(s) - 1`; FE reflection sends `a` to `-a`. Gamma-six residue, Möbius prime-normal growth, Green codiagonal, Wold/Fisher response, affine Xi residue, several Hellinger/Kakutani readers recover `a`, `a²`, or its support.
- F06 FE transports, does not select: common recovery lifts FE without information or formation-capital loss; FE-symmetric entire positive countermodels can have off-line zeros.
- F07 Faithful recovery conserves obstruction: split/injective recovery preserves information, Hellinger affinity, formation capital, completion boundary; strict improvement requires erasure.
- F07a CANNOT count copied labels as independently formed data: diagonal copying preserves every coordinate marginal but = independent `d`-fold product (`d>=2`) only for a Dirac packet, even for normalized signed real weights. Two finite probability packets: one common stochastic map produces both independent products ⇔ packets identical or disjoint supports. [L] `finiteDiagonalPower_marginal`, `finiteDiagonalPower_eq_independentPower_iff_dirac`, `finiteFormationClone_exists_iff`. Matching marginals / faithful split recovery do not give that replicator; cloning argument MUST construct it.
- F08 Formation and completion do not commute: off-line current appears as completion boundary, Roos `lim¹`, physical transport; boundary absence RH⇔; finite formation never sees the class.
- F09 Bounded presentation changes are genuine gauges: mutually bounded dictionaries preserve saturation and completion boundary; ordinary naturality across bounded gauges cannot annihilate off-line class.
- F10 Quotients erase typed data differently: positive squares erase sign/phase; reduced quotients nilpotent tangents; scalar fusion homology; compact observation lifted history.
- F10a MUST distinguish global phase choice from relative-phase information: infinite locally finite connected graph: Boolean edge coherences mod finite defects determine vertex phases mod finite changes + one global flip ⇔ every finite-edge-boundary cut has a finite side; products of two infinite connected graphs satisfy it. [L] `CoherenceTailQuotient.coherenceTailMap_injective_iff_finiteBoundaryOneEnded`, `CoherenceEndReconstruction.finiteBoundaryOneEnded_boxProd` (namespace `HTGeometry`). Finite-boundary cuts mod finite sets = exactly clopen end-space ring (symmetric-difference addition, intersection multiplication): `CoherenceEndClopen.finiteBoundaryBooleanRingQuotientEquivClopens`. Topology/reconstruction laws; not arithmetic sum/product, not RH selector.
- F11 Observer recurrence ≠ source return: compact observer can recur while source lift escapes linearly; faithful recurrence descent = RH receiver.
- F12 Hypertranscendence gives independent directions and noncompactness, not vanishing; unbounded rank/depth necessary for several completion births, insufficient for selection.
- F13 Provenance and multiplicity survive only in typed landings: prime labels, valuation depth, Xi multiplicity, reflection orientation, ancestry cannot be replaced by total mass or norm.
- F14 External polarization can keep what internal multiplication erases: reflected pairing cancels signed current, doubles unsigned escape; higher arity alone does not select.
- F15 Escape conserved as capacity in proved models: visible cancellation moves exact energy to unobserved ports; any proposed annihilation needs closed accounting identity.
- F16 Quantization records, does not prohibit: integer, rank, `π`-scaled residues encode multiplicity, do not imply absence.
- F16a MUST distinguish arbitrary finite depth from a finite source cutoff: holomorphic function nonzero somewhere on connected open domain with holomorphic `2^k`-th root on whole domain ∀ `k` ⇒ nonvanishing (positive finite zero order not divisible by every `2^k`); no root compatibility or limit needed; simply connected domain ⇒ coherent tower. Unproved arithmetic input = global finite-depth roots, not coherence. [L] `RHTower.CoherentTower.nonvanishing_of_exists_roots_all_two_powers_preconnected`, `coherentTower_iff_exists_roots_all_two_powers_preconnected`.
- F16b CANNOT infer integral vanishing from all modular observations without excluding divisible components: adjoining a nonzero rational vector space to an abelian group preserves mod-`m` quotient data + torsion subgroup; every hom to a direct sum of finite groups annihilates it. Finite generation adds information: non-torsion ⇒ surjection to ℤ. [L] `HTGeometry.DivisibilityFirewall.divisibility_firewall`, `exists_int_surjection_of_not_isTorsion`.
- F16c MUST derive invertibility on the actual obstruction space when using finite phase transport to force integer vanishing: coefficient space of dimension `d>0`, clock entries `1,z,…,z^(d−1)`, shift with closing-edge weight `h`: `CS=zSC` ⇔ `h=0` or `z^d=1`; zero-edge shift always works but singular. Primitive `2^k`-th root: invertible pair exists ⇔ `2^k | d` (converse constructs it). Corpus's actual off-critical zero-multiplicity spaces: existence at cofinally many depths ⇔ RH. Identifies what an arithmetic invertible transport must prove. [L] `dyadicBoundaryCompletedJetWeyl_iff_zero_or_dvd`, `boundaryCompletedJetShift_zero_det`, `semDyadicWeylModel_nonempty_iff_pow_dvd_holeCount`, `riemannHypothesis_iff_cofinal_semDyadicWeylModels`.
- F17 Cofinal order is law data: fixed cutoffs, convergent scalar prefixes, compact subsequences, local positivity do not control moving heads, apertures, domains, retained infinite tails.
- F18 No-go survivors are systematic: completion boundary, connecting rank, nilpotent tangent, signed orientation, unobserved capacity, quantized residue, unbounded domain, singular comparison; calling all of them curvature loses information.

### Euler-deletion paths, refinement and chronological sums
Notation: deletion coefficient `a_P(n)=1-P[P|n]`; deletion source `E_P(s,n)=a_P(n)n^(-s)`.
- CANNOT infer an arithmetic constraint from counting cancellations: [L] two zero-marginal sources (dyadic–triadic deletion grid, all row/column sums 0) keep a nonzero multiplication-fibre interaction (collapsing `(m,n)` by `mn` changes squared mass `12→10`, defect 2), but the full cancellation count of every fixed fibre equals eight shifted divisor counts (Dirichlet series = fixed polynomial × `ζ²`); an origin-labelled block moment is not intrinsic orientation (cyclic origin change alters the integer first moment; apparent charge 0 in `ZMod 6`). A decaying solution of `f(pq)-f(p)-Af(q)+Af(1)=0` (`|A|≥1`) is 0 ⇒ at every zero with `0<σ≤1` some `q` has `W_(pq)-W_p-p^(2-2σ)W_q+p^(2-2σ)W_1≠0` (nonvanishing side of a possible contradiction; a strict-right-only vanishing theorem still needs independent content). For `C(s)=Σμ(p)W_p/[ps̄(s̄+1)]`, `Q(s)=-1/[2s̄(1-s̄)]`, `C-Q=-conj(ζ(s)/(s(s+1)))`: every neighbourhood of a strip zero contains nonzeros with `Re C=Re Q`, and any strip zero forces an off-line nonzero with it; the omitted imaginary part at a zero is `Im(s)(2Re(s)-1)/(2|s̄(1-s̄)|²)`. A nonzero higher defect exists only when composition acts on labels: `K_α=D_α(P,Q)+D_α(PQ,R)-D_α(Q,R)-D_α(P,QR)=Z(α)(Q·Q^(-α))(1-P·P^(-α))(Q-1)(1-R^(2-α))` (negative on `1<α<2`, zero exactly at `α=2`). MUST: a schedule selector starts at depth ≥ 3 with a factorization-tree grouping law not fixed by quasi-shuffle associativity; an order-sensitive proposal needs defined execution-history data (same final stream ⇒ same every function of it). ### Energy bookkeeping for signed prime schedules (CCM-FU)
All [L] on the literal source, no numerical premises; cards in P3/P5 named `ccm-fu-*`.
- MUST keep overlap corrections: the squared size of a sum includes `2Re` of cross products; dropping them or replacing components by independent copies changes the measured quantity; a quotient declaring them zero needs a compatible observation map. A bound on the sum of component energies leaves total interference; nonpositive totals do not make components nonpositive (explicit components/aggregate of opposite sign, positive contributions unbounded); growing nonnegative components say nothing about the signed combination. (MUST-217)
- CANNOT impose a common intermediate and final bound when the exact difference contains positive mass growing faster than the bound; quantify cancellation at the final scale. A global energy bound does not give an ordering of pair mergers with nonincreasing energy: one exact finite source violates the ceiling in every order despite positive unsigned coefficients, contracting first steps and a final energy below the diagonal (P5:ccm-fu-positive-prime-cube-ceiling). (MUST-217)
- MUST include the squared mean in averaged budgets (mean square − squared mean = variance; P3:ccm-fu-bernoulli-prime-energy-identities); cannot repair by a larger constant or dropping a prefix when mean/scale is unbounded on every tail (P3:ccm-fu-half-selection-growth-obstruction); for one-sign scalar blocks endpoint energies control every intermediate subset (head crosses 0 at most once; P3:ccm-fu-late-prime-block-endpoints). (MUST-217)
- MUST recompute orientation after source-preserving mergers (a rescaled copy keeps its own sign changes; keep the mixed inner product; P3:ccm-fu-prime-fibre-recursion); compare sources in the same retained-label state with the actual integration clock and partial boundary blocks (P3:ccm-fu-prime-fibre-interpolation); support of interactions ≠ energy reserve — every next move can raise energy while both complete orders fit the budget, and the minimum achievable peak includes the minimum next energy (P5:ccm-fu-overlap-phase-contraction). (MUST-RETAINED-SOURCE-ORIENTATION, MUST-CLIPPED-SOURCE-COMPATIBILITY, MUST-OVERLAP-ENERGY-RESERVE)
- MUST budget total displacement: with head square bounded by the energy, two endpoint ceilings `B` limit one-sign block mass to `2√B` ⇒ if required mass grows faster than `√B` the number of blocks must grow (no fixed-block reordering; P5:ccm-fu-fixed-block-prime-schedules); removing a head square does not dissipate remaining energy — keep `2×` signed overlaps and the absorbed square; the sign of a head increment decides whether consuming the same mass helps (P5:ccm-fu-nonempty-energy-contraction); total-energy contraction does not give a move dissipating outside a distinguished component (P3:ccm-fu-transverse-dissipation-trap). (MUST-GROWING-INTERLEAVING-CAPACITY, MUST-NONEMPTY-ENERGY-COUPLING, MUST-ALLOW-ENERGY-TRANSFER)
- MUST select continuable states: fixed positive weightings admit complete decreasing evolutions only on disjoint weight intervals for two states of one source; keep weights uniformly away from 0 (P3:ccm-fu-weighted-head-state-selection). A discrete correction can release the whole head square (crossing selection with excess ≤ one component, paid by consumed squares; P3:ccm-fu-late-prime-head-neutralization), but only linear reserve if components are `O(√cutoff)` (P3:ccm-fu-uniform-channel-square-root-bound); contraction of one step must preserve the orientation the next step needs (alternating signs by cardinality make each merger contract, later ones need not; P3:ccm-fu-oriented-initial-prime-extension, P3:ccm-fu-two-channel-prime-blocks); budget aligned and reversed-sign energies (geometric-mean gain bound; P3:ccm-fu-parity-defect-transport). (MUST-SELECT-CONTINUABLE-STATES, MUST-DISCRETE-CORRECTION-RESERVE, MUST-PRESERVE-NEXT-STEP-ORIENTATION, MUST-QUANTIFY-CORRECTION-RESERVE-SCALE, MUST-BUDGET-SIGN-DEFECT-COUPLING)
- MUST distinguish scalar completion from overlapping scheduling (absorbing isolated coefficients into one real head: an adaptive order keeps every intermediate energy below the larger endpoint, whatever the signs; endpoints still to be proved; P3:ccm-fu-square-root-terminal-order); control amplitudes as well as signs (nonnegative `2×2` array with `b,c≤2a`, `e≤2b,2c` bounds the alternating square by the sum of squares; 2 is sharp; P3:ccm-fu-two-prime-local-criterion); retain the mean of a discrete primitive before summing by parts again (zero-mean periodic source, nonzero-mean primitive; P3:ccm-fu-second-centered-hyperbola); quantify tails uniformly in cutoff and dilation and keep moving boundary profiles (P3:ccm-fu-smooth-square-block-mean, P3:ccm-fu-large-prime-orientation; middle profiles open); specify separators from source data and keep signed mixed products of affine families (P3:ccm-fu-quotient-window-orders); change inherited nonempty coordinates before an unbounded lower bound forms (P5:ccm-fu-increasing-middle-scalar-reserves); prove both direction and availability from finite pools (P3:ccm-fu-coupled-prime-batches). (MUST-SCALAR-TERMINAL-ENDPOINTS, MUST-LOCAL-AMPLITUDE-COMPARISON, MUST-RECENTER-DISCRETE-PRIMITIVES, MUST-UNIFORM-DILATION-TAILS, MUST-SOURCE-FIXED-SEPARATORS, MUST-CONTROL-INHERITED-NONEMPTY-ENERGY, MUST-PAIR-DIRECTION-WITH-AVAILABILITY)
- MUST identify the operation that carries curvature and prove it is the one controlled by the analytic closure data: [L] centrally lifting an associative label product by a binary correction `D` gives associator = four-term curvature of `D`; for `(P,a)⋆(Q,b)=(PQ,a+b+D_α(P,Q))` the associator is `K_α(P,Q,R)`, nonassociative for pairwise-coprime `P,Q,R>1` on `1<α<2`. A sign-definite nonzero invariant is not a contradiction: the object's defining closure must independently force the same invariant to vanish; associativity of the base operation cannot justify equality of trees after a non-cocyclic correction; annihilating the curvature on a special fibre needs an observation/quotient admitting the correction, derived from the fibre's closure data. - CANNOT select the line by existence/invertibility of ordered products that exist on a region crossing it: [L] products of `exp(n^(-s)(a_2(n)X+a_3(n)Y))` converge to a unit for every `Re s>1/5`, arbitrary (noncommuting) `X,Y` in a complete normed unital algebra, every prefix; for one explicit target convergence on the real axis holds exactly above `1/5`. - MUST keep the original series convergent when changing weights for a countermodel: [L] multiplying the weight at `p^km` (`p∤m`) by `c^k` (`0<c<1`) keeps positivity and complete multiplicativity but makes the unchanged deletion series diverge for `Re s<1`; the unique bounded-prefix repair replaces `p` by `p/c` (converges for `Re s>0`) and changes the source operation; with that compensation the whole sign boundary of the quadratic obstruction is preserved and, with the first two test weights 1, obstruction/mass is exactly unchanged ⇒ shrinking inputs and reusing the old comparison supplies nothing. P3:euler-deletion-linear-span-audit. (CANNOT-LIN1)
- MUST apply rank constraints to the actual accumulated quantity: an endpoint wedge has rank ≤ 2 but an ordered area sums wedges over many time pairs; [N] computations at three approximate zeros strongly disfavor a rank-two area law (diagnostic, not a certified counterexample). No FE imposed in any of these; a proof MUST use a proved constraint on the full literal sequence (tail bound or FE). - CANNOT escape periodic coefficients by nonlinear rules on finitely many fixed congruence tests of `an+b` (result depends only on `n` modulo the product of moduli ⇒ finite Hurwitz-zeta combination); growing moduli, operations on summed functions, ordered infinite histories not covered. ### Jensen polynomials, integrated prime error, central moments
- CANNOT certify a quartic Jensen polynomial from independent bounds on the three dispersions `D_l=m(n+l)m(n+l+2)/m(n+l+1)²−1`: along the equal-dispersion (log-normal) diagonal the tolerance is `≍1/b` (`b=n+½`; the log-normal reference is a rescaled Hermite quartic, hyperbolic for `D·b<1`), transverse to it (`D_{n+1}` alone, i.e. the second difference `D₀−2D₁+D₂`) only `≍1/b²` (perturbation terms `≍b⁴|ε|` against reference `≍b²`) ⇒ box sandwiches on moment ratios are useless at degree four. A dispersion certificate MUST give two-sided control of the size-biased theta dispersions to relative precision `O(1/n)` localised at the tilted mode (`Var(log s)·b≤1/2`, `|κ₃|≲b^{−3/2}`, `|κ₄|≲b^{−2}` for `x^{2κ}Φ(x)dx`, `κ≥n`); curvature-infimum bounds cannot (the infimum is 0 in the log variable); with these and `b≥2000` the quartic is hyperbolic [L]. (CANNOT-321, MUST-224)
- CANNOT individuate zeros in the Jensen–Pólya regime `d=o(n)`: [literature] for `n³log²(n+2)≥Kd⁵` all `J^{d,n}` of `Ξ` are hyperbolic unconditionally (Holland, arXiv:2608.08682; five Taylor ratios matched by a real-rooted finite-free product of two Jacobi factors; semicircle zero law); input: sectorial saddle asymptotic of `log M_z` (`M_z=∫Φ(u)u^{2z}du`; derivatives `2/(x𝓛_x)`, `−2/(x²𝓛_x)`, `4/(x³𝓛_x)`, `x=L(πe^L+¾)`) ⇒ `D₀b→0` like `2/log n`, `D₀−D₁≍2/(n²log n)`, `D₀−2D₁+D₂≍2/(n³log n)`; finite-order matching stops below `d≍n log n` (residual `≍(d+2√(nd))^{2K+1}/(n^{2K}(2K+1)!log n)`). RH information sits only at `d≳n`: all `J^{d,n}` for one `n` hyperbolic ⇔ `F₀^{(n)}` Laguerre–Pólya (`Ξ(t)=F₀(−t²)`), which for some `n` ⇔ finitely many non-real zeros (Pólya–Wiman, Fourier–Pólya for order `<2`) ⇒ the first non-hyperbolic degree satisfies `d*(n)≥cn^{3/5}log^{2/5}(n+2)`; a producer MUST control Taylor coefficients below the saddle threshold jointly with the asymptotic ones; the perturbative wedge is RH-blind. (CANNOT-322, MUST-225)
- MUST (and need only) prove a uniform square-root bound for the signed integrated prime error: [L] `D(X)=X−1−Σ_{n≤X}Λ(n)log(X/n)`, any finite `|D(X)|≤B√X` (`X≥1`) ⇔ RH; no small `B` required; a logarithmic loss fails the criterion; unsigned prime terms and formal source identities do not supply the signed bound. (CANNOT-323, MUST-226)
- MUST know negative central moment witnesses have degree ≥ 2: [L] `−Ξ'(t)/(tΞ(t))=ΣM_kt^(2k)` satisfies `a²M₀+2abM₁+b²M₂≥0` unconditionally (exact theta-source integration; R:ThetaGibbsSteinSourceMinor). (CANNOT-324)
- MUST control the exponent, not constants, in the fixed exponential-pole criterion: its zero margin is a constant-level artefact of test transforms with real zeros; aperture-window averages `∫_a^{a+1}E_±` remain RH⇔ (an off-line zero contributes `e^{2aβ₀}`× an oscillating sign-changing factor) and under RH are `≥0.94·c₀(e−1)e^a` (cross term `≤(e+1)Σ|ρ|^{−3}√X≈0.004√X` vs mean `≈0.079√X`); every equivalent form is von Koch's statement that `Σ_{n≤e^h}Λ(n)/√n−2e^{h/2}` grows at most polynomially in `h` (functionals against `K_a'`, `K_a(h)=4sinh(a−h/2)+2(2a−h)cosh(h/2)`). [N] with 1500 zeros the worst relative margin over `a≤A` is `0.42` (`A=10³`), `0.33/0.31` (`A=10⁶`), ≈`0.5−0.013 log A`, `>0.48` at `a=log p/2`, `p≤10⁸` ⇒ a proof losing a fixed factor below 3 is not excluded by constants; the exponent `Θ=½` excludes every known argument (sieve, mean value, positivity). (MUST-227, MUST-228)

### The Weil lag function (exact form of RH as one bounded function)
Notes: P1:integer-lag-law-notes, P1:lag-ratio-law.
`Φ(L)=Σ'_ρ e^{L(ρ−½)}/(ρ(1−ρ))` (absolutely convergent for all real `L`, even, continuous); `Φ(0)=a_1=2+γ−log 4π=Σ'_ρ1/(ρ(1−ρ))` ≈ 0.046 (= first Li coefficient `λ₁`) [L]. Prime side [L]: for `X=e^L>1`, `Σ'_ρX^ρ/(ρ(1−ρ))=(X−ψ(X))+X(Σ_{n≤X}Λ(n)/n−log X+γ)−log 2π+S(X)`, `S(X)=Σ_{k≥1}X^{−2k}/(2k(2k+1))`; equivalently `Φ=Φ_arch+Σ_{n≤X}2Λ(n)n^{−1/2}sinh((L−log n)/2)` (archimedean germ + one convex kink of slope `Λ(n)/√n` per prime power); integer lags `a_n=Φ(log n)`; `√xΦ(log x)=1+γ−log 2π+∫_1^x m(t)dt+S(x)` with Mertens error `m(t)=Σ_{n≤t}Λ(n)/n−log t+γ`.
- MUST prove boundedness of `Φ`; every form is Lean-equivalent to RH, with the sharp constant built in: RH ⇔ `Φ` bounded ⇔ bounded above ⇔ bounded below ⇔ `|Φ|≤Φ(0)` ⇔ `Φ` positive-definite ⇔ `Φ` Bohr almost periodic ⇔ `∀n: a_n≤a_1` ⇔ `(a_n)` bounded above ⇔ bounded below ⇔ every arithmetic Gram matrix `(Φ(log(n/m)))_{n,m≤N}` PSD (2×2 minors on `{1,n}` are `2(a_1−a_n)`) ⇔ `Φ` bounded above on the `{p,q}`-rationals `p^kq^l` (any two primes; dense lags) ⇔ `a_p≤a_1` at all primes ⇔ bounded above on prime powers ⇔ on `k`-th powers (any `k≥1`) ⇔ Cramér's mean-square criterion `∫_1^X(ψ(t)−t)²t^{−2}dt=O(log X)` (under RH `Φ` = mean autocorrelation of `Ψ(x)=e^{−x/2}(e^x−ψ(e^x))`). Dichotomy [L]: either every `a_n∈[−a_1,a_1]` (RH) or `(a_n)` is unbounded above and below — no intermediate regime; boundedness by any `M` gives the sharp `M=Φ(0)` ("losing a constant costs nothing, losing the exponent is impossible"). Mechanism of converses: Landau (a one-sided bound makes `M∓Φ` a nonnegative density whose Laplace transform `(M+1)/s∓G(s)`, `G(s)=Σ(ρ(1−ρ))⁻¹/(s−(ρ−½))`, is analytic at every real point, so its abscissa is 0) + a zero of maximal real part on its horizontal line giving a pole with pole-free right ray. (MUST-232, MUST-235, MUST-236, MUST-237, MUST-238, MUST-240, MUST-243, MUST-244, MUST-251, MUST-252, MUST-254)
- MUST have zero margin: [L] under RH `a_1` is the exact supremum of `{a_n:n≥N}` for every `N`, approached along the powers of every base (Dirichlet approximation of finitely many `γlog b/2π`); unconditionally `limsup a_n≥a_1` (equality iff RH) ⇒ no proof can lose any constant along any `b^m`, and a proof must control the joint phases `γlog b` exactly. The floor is arithmetic: under RH `inf a_n=−a_1` ⇔ the ordinates have no odd relation (every integer relation `Σc_γγ=0` has even coefficient sum; weaker than LI); only `inf a_n<0` is forced. [L] Unconditional `Ω±(√n)`: the genus-kernel error `E(n)=√n a_n` exceeds `(a_1−ε)√n` and is `≤−η√n` infinitely often ⇒ the sign of `E(n)` is no producer; only the size of positive excursions is RH-sensitive. (MUST-239, MUST-240, MUST-241)
- MUST produce marginal, gapless positivity exactly at the true prime data: under RH on every window `[0,L]` the Toeplitz operator of `Φ` is `Σ_ρ|ρ|^{−2}|e^{iγx}><e^{iγx}|` (trace `LΦ(0)`, spectrum accumulating at 0, density `~log ξ/(2πξ²)`); moving or rescaling one kink by `ε≠0` adds a signed density `~εΛ(n)n^{−1/2}sin(ξ log n)/ξ` exceeding `log ξ/ξ²` at high frequency ⇒ not positive-definite for any `ε≠0` [theorem under RH; N]. [N] Keeping only prime powers `≤P`, positive-definiteness fails at `L=log n₁+δ`, `δ∈[0.013,0.05]` (`n₁` the next prime power; 42 truncations `P≤2048`); `|Φ_P|≤Φ(0)` fails at `log n₁+δ'`, `δ'≤2arcsinh(Φ(0)√n₁/Λ(n₁))`. ⇒ CANNOT be a producer with a uniform gap (coercivity, strict Hankel/Toeplitz PSD with margin, strictly positive symbol); CANNOT treat any finite set of primes as negligible or replace prime powers by any approximation, however accurate: positivity holds only for exact positions `log n` and weights `Λ(n)/√n`. [N] `λ_min(G_N)·N≈0.084→0.089` (`N=50…3200`; `λ_max/N→1/(¼+γ₁²)`), below the cusp bound `(log N)/(2πN)` by a `log N` ⇒ a producer must deliver a quantitative Hodge-index margin `≍1/N` on the Frobenius span; an off-line zero at height `T` moves `G_N` by `≤N²log T/(πT)` ⇒ numerical PSD checks never outrun the verified zero-free height. P2:computations/lag-function-pd-horizon, P2:computations/lag-function-pd-rigidity, P2:computations/lag-gram-margin. (MUST-233, MUST-234, MUST-245)
- MUST supply an archimedean Hodge index: the criterion is the Weil bound `|N(X)−M(X)|≤c₀√X` (`N(X)=Σ_{n≤X}Λ(n)(1+1/n)log(X/n)`) with the regularised genus `c₀=Σ1/(ρ(1−ρ))` in the role of `2g` — the shape `|#C(F_q)−(q+1)|≤2g√q` whose only known producer is the Hodge index theorem on `C×C`. A geometric producer MUST give an intersection pairing on principal divisors of `Spec ℤ` whose regularised values are `Φ(log(n/m))` (Frobenius-graph pairings weighted by `1/(ρ(1−ρ))`) with Hodge inequality exactly `Φ(0)≥Φ(L)`, tight along every geometric progression; on the zero carrier the kernel is the Gram form of the Frobenius orbit `v_x(ρ)=x^{ρ−½}` under `B(f,g)=Σf(ρ)g(1−ρ)/(ρ(1−ρ))` ([L] `B(v_x,v_y)=Φ(log x−log y)` unconditionally; RH ⇔ `B≥0` on that span) ⇒ the reflection `ρ↦1−ρ` must act as a conjugation on a positive form (Hodge-star/Frobenius compatibility). Weaker positivity (up to a constant, on a subgroup with gap) does not reach RH. (MUST-229, MUST-231, MUST-243)
- CANNOT locate the obstruction in self-pairings: [L] `A(a)e^{−a}→2+γ−log 4π>0.011` (Mertens with Euler constant + PNT with error) ⇒ pole self-energies are positive at large apertures; `Q(p_±)` sees an off-line `β` only as `e^{a(2β−1)}=o(e^a)` (PNT-level); the entire RH content is the cross pairing `Re Q(p₊,p₋)=Σ_ρX^ρ/ρ²+O(1)` — RH is Cauchy–Schwarz `|Q(p₊,p₋)|²≤Q(p₊)Q(p₋)` for this one pair at every aperture. Exponents are not confined to reflection pairings: `Q(f_a,f_b)=2cosh((a+b)/2)Φ(a−b)−2cosh((a−b)/2)Φ(a+b)` ⇒ same-orientation pairings see `e^{(a−b)(β−½)}`, reflection pairings `e^{(a+b)(β−½)}`; only the diagonal `a=b` is blind ⇒ a producer may be a statement about lags between apertures of one orientation. (CANNOT-324r, MUST-230, CANNOT-325)
- CANNOT reach the lag criterion with known tools; its open content is one one-sided prime inequality at scale `√X`: `√X∫_X^∞(ψ(t)−t+log 2π+½log(1−t^{−2}))t^{−2}dt≥−C` (future primes never too sparse), or the reverse (never too dense) — either half alone is RH; at primes: `√p∫_p^∞(t−ψ(t))t^{−2}dt≤2+γ−log 4π+o(1)`; in Mertens form `∫_1^p m(t)dt≤C√p`. Sieves bound primes from above; de la Vallée Poussin positivity (`Λ≥0` × positive trigonometric polynomial at `σ>1`) and exponential-sum methods give scale `X exp(−c(log X)^{3/5})`; lower-bound sieves rest on Bombieri–Vinogradov (errors `N/log^AN`); mean-value theorems for `(1/T)∫_0^TΦ²` need Dirichlet polynomials of length `e^T`. A new route MUST supply a new one-sided bound for primes in `[X/c,cX]` at scale `√X`, not another equivalent form. (CANNOT-326)

### Constrained Weil form: exact rates and detection limits
Screw form `Q(φ)=Σ_ρ c̄_ρ(φ)c_ρ(φ)/z_ρ²` (`z_ρ=−i(ρ−½)`); `Θ_sup=sup|Re ρ−½|`; `μ₀(B)` = pole-vanishing (restricted) ground energy at aperture `B` (RH ⇔ `μ₀≥0` for all `B`, Bombieri–Yoshida; `μ₀` has at most one zero aperture, positive below, negative above).

A producer must control exactly the defect sum. (MUST-265)

Prime side: the source problem is exactly the exponential growth rate of the pole-compressed prime translation operator `P_{V₀}(Σ_nΛ(n)n^{−½}τ_{log n})P_{V₀}` on `L²(−A,A)`: trivial rate `e^A` (`‖T‖≤Σ_{n<e^{2A}}Λ(n)n^{−½}~2e^A`), rate `<1` by any margin = zero-free strip, sub-exponential = RH; no unconditional estimate below rate 1 is known (termwise bounds reproduce the trivial rate). [L] Equivalently, the hinge sum `H(t)=Σ_{2≤n≤e^t}Λ(n)n^{−½}(t−log n)` vs Suzuki's smooth part `M(t)=4e^{t/2}+4e^{−t/2}−8+(c₁/2)t+c₀−ℓ(t)`: `H≤M+Ce^{εt}` on `t≥0` ⇔ every `|Re ρ−½|≤ε` (exact rate; `C=2Σ|ρ−½|⁻²` at `ε=Θ_sup`); only an upper bound on the prime sum and one-sided sub-exponential room are needed; trivial `ε=½`; the two-sided pointwise `ψ(x)−x=O(x^{½+ε})` is stronger than needed. ### Exact prime weights are load-bearing (restricted Weil operator)
- MUST use every prime with its exact von Mangoldt weight: [L] deleting one prime `p₀∈(3 594 641,7 189 282]`, or any prime `p≥e^{18}`, makes the explicit-formula functional negative on a two-bump test `φ(·−u₀/2)−φ(·+u₀/2)` (`u₀=log p`) ⇒ no argument whose inputs survive a single-prime deletion (exact primes up to a threshold, `0≤Λ≤log`, Chebyshev capacities, tail bounds, averaged statistics) can prove Weil positivity (e.g. arXiv 2609.20367 is refuted as written); the Connes–Consani–Moscovici restricted Weil ground energy is continuous antitone in the aperture for every aperture-compatible bounded self-adjoint remainder family, so a sign change is a genuine zero crossing with nonzero kernel; the `p`-deleted operator, the operator with any fictitious block `w·x^{−1/2}(τ_{log x}+τ_{−log x})` (`x≥2`, any `w≠0`; any `x>1` for `w>0`; i.e. any `Λ(p)∈[log p−ε,log p]`), and the prime-free operator (archimedean multiplier integral `∫2γ(2πξ)|û|²` negative on wide bumps, `≤−cL⁻³+CL⁻⁴`) all cross zero while every structural input of the lane (positive atomic jump form, self-adjointness, boundedness, half-log traces) holds for them. Rigidity: for finitely many distinct scales `x_i>1` or countably many with `Σ|w_n|(2/√x_n)(1+log x_n)<∞`, positivity at all apertures ⇔ RH ∧ all `w=0` (dense gap frequencies + uniqueness for nonpositive cosine series). Open core: heavy perturbations `Σ|w_n|log(x_n)/√x_n=∞` (e.g. deleting a residue class of primes). ⇒ no sieve majorant/minorant, smoothed prime measure or approximate weights can replace `Λ`; `Λ` must vanish off prime powers exactly as much as `Λ(p)=log p`. (MUST-357, MUST-358, MUST-359, MUST-360, MUST-361, MUST-362, MUST-363, MUST-372, MUST-373)

### Hadamard receiver `Re(ξ'/ξ)≥0` and the Laguerre margin

Reflection pairing gives the weaker unconditional tail law (copies with `|γ|≥(|Im s|+1)/(Re s−½)+1` are nonnegative) ⇒ the receiver is local in height, no tail difficulty exists. The remaining obstruction is exactly local: a copy with `|β−½|>Re s−½` makes its own pair term negative near its pole. [L] Consequence: `|ξ(σ+it)|` is increasing in `σ` on `(½,∞)` for `|t|≤4` and on `[1−1/(58 log(|t|+½)),∞)` for `|t|>4` (the Sondow–Dumitrescu form needs a uniform half-plane zero bound and unconditionally reaches only `Re s≥1`). ### Divisor-function families, source deformations and rigidity
- MUST know the twisted divisor analytic layer (R:EstermannSmoothedSum and its Estermann*/Rectangle*/Hurwitz strip-bound modules): [L] for every modulus `q`, unit `a`, `x>0`: `Σ_{N≥1}d(N)e(aN/q)e^{−Nx}=(γ−log x−2log q)/(qx)+(1/2π)∫x^{−(½+iv)}Γ(½+iv)D(½+iv,a/q)dv` with error `≤C_{q,a}x^{−1/2}`; Estermann `D(s,a/q)`: FE, `(s−1)²D` entire, `D=q^{1−2s}ζ²+q^{−s}R_a`, principal part `1/q`, `(2γ−2log q)/q`, polynomial strip bounds. The FE acts on the family through inversion of residues: in the additive Fourier basis entries `q⁻¹S(m,−n;q)` (size `2q^{−1/2}` off the zero frequency, `1` at it) ⇒ the symmetry is flat except in the one row carrying ζ (at modulus 1 it is a scalar, hence level-one symmetry carries no zero information); [P] transporting the flat part gives the additive-twist average at prime modulus left-edge exponent `7/2` (vs 4) and critical exponent `9/4` (vs `5/2`), a saving `q^{1/4}`. - MUST obtain RH from a rigidity, never from an estimate: every condition set either admits an object with an off-line zero or already determines ζ. Explicit witness: `d(N)−2^θd(N/2)` (even `N`) stays within `1+2^θ` of `d(N)` (keeping the full Kloosterman-weighted square-root cancellation) and gives `ζ(s)²(1−2^{θ−s})`, vanishing at `s=θ` for every real `θ`; iterating one Euler-factor step at one prime realizes any finite list of prescribed zeros with coefficients `≪d(N)` ⇒ no inequality whose hypotheses survive a single-prime Euler-factor deformation can give RH, nor even a zero-free region or a count of off-line zeros; the missing statement must have no slack, with Dirichlet series and FE acting together (classical: a Dirichlet series with ζ's FE is ζ). Third case: a condition set can define a large class all of whose members have critical zeros (real-rooted entire functions, Hermite–Biehler class, self-adjoint spectra, zeta functions of curves over `F_q` — produced by construction: cohomology + nondegenerate pairing forcing |Frobenius roots|, never by testing). No such class is known for ζ and it cannot be reached by verification ⇒ MUST construct a category in which ζ is one member among many, carrying the pairing, whose regularization preserves the positivity that is free before it, and whose membership test has a deformation space that is neither everything nor a point. (MUST-389, MUST-390)
- CANNOT exclude off-line zeros from finite source data + the real divisor: for each `m≥1`, `k_t=k+t·k^{(4m)}` (`k` the theta source) is positive for small `t>0`, keeps moments through order `4m−1` and every real Fourier zero with multiplicity, strongly log-concave, Schwartz, every-rate decay, yet its transform `XiC(z)(1+tz^{4m})` has a nonreal packet at radius `t^{−1/(4m)}`; an even positive strongly log-concave compact perturbation equal to theta on both tails (finite Gram correction of moments) still has a nonreal zero (Rouché) ⇒ tails alone do not link to the interior; no finite family of ambient inequalities continuous in exponentially weighted derivative seminorms and strict at theta can certify real-rootedness (the packets converge to `k` in all seminorms) ⇒ MUST use a boundary equality, a nonuniform/cofinal/singular observation, or an exact arithmetic membership law.
